027252:     function getSelectedTrainedPersonnelCountForCode(
027253:         selectedVehicles,
027254:         trainingCode
027255:     ) {
027256:         const code = String(trainingCode || '').trim();
027257:         if (!code) return 0;
027258: 
027259:         return (Array.isArray(selectedVehicles) ? selectedVehicles : [])
027260:             .reduce((total, vehicle) => {
027261:                 if (vehicle?.profilesComplete) {
027262:                     return total + vehicle.profiles.reduce(
027263:                         (vehicleTotal, profile) => {
027264:                             return vehicleTotal + (
027265:                                 Array.isArray(profile) &&
027266:                                 profile.includes(code)
027267:                                     ? 1
027268:                                     : 0
027269:                             );
027270:                         },
027271:                         0
027272:                     );
027273:                 }
027274: 
027275:                 const aggregate =
027276:                     (Array.isArray(vehicle?.trainingCounts)
027277:                         ? vehicle.trainingCounts
027278:                         : []
027279:                     ).find(item => item.code === code);
027280: 
027281:                 return total + Math.max(
027282:                     0,
027283:                     parseInt(aggregate?.count, 10) || 0
027284:                 );
027285:             }, 0);
027286:     }

007318:     function getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(personnel, vehicle) {
007319:         if (!Array.isArray(personnel) || !vehicle) return [];
007320: 
007321:         const index = getPersonnelAssignmentIndex(personnel);
007322:         const vehicleId = String(vehicle.vehicleId || '');
007323:         const vehicleNameKey = normalizePersonnelVehicleName(vehicle.name || '');
007324:         const byId = vehicleId ? (index.byVehicleId.get(vehicleId) || []) : [];
007325:         const byName = vehicleNameKey ? (index.byVehicleName.get(vehicleNameKey) || []) : [];
007326: 
007327:         if (!byId.length) return byName;
007328:         if (!byName.length || byName === byId) return byId;
007329: 
007330:         const result = byId.slice();
007331:         const seen = new Set(result.map(person => String(person.personnelId || '')));
007332:         byName.forEach(person => {
007333:             const personnelId = String(person.personnelId || '');
007334:             if (!seen.has(personnelId)) {
007335:                 seen.add(personnelId);
007336:                 result.push(person);
007337:             }
007338:         });
007339:         return result;
007340:     }

007522:     function sortPersonnelCandidatesForRule(candidates, rule) {
007523:         return candidates.slice().sort((left, right) => {
007524:             const leftReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => left.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007525:             const rightReserved = rule.preferWithout.some(code => right.trainingCodes.includes(code)) ? 1 : 0;
007526:             if (leftReserved !== rightReserved) return leftReserved - rightReserved;
007527: 
007528:             const extraDifference = left.trainingCodes.length - right.trainingCodes.length;
007529:             if (extraDifference) return extraDifference;
007530:             return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'en-GB', { numeric: true });
007531:         });
007532:     }

014898:     function isVerifiedCriticalCareAmbulanceRegistryEntry(entry) {
014899:         if (!entry || entry.assignmentScanComplete !== true) return false;
014900:         if (String(entry.vehicleTypeId || '') !== '5') return false;
014901: 
014902:         const updatedAt = Number(entry.updatedAt || 0);
014903:         if (
014904:             !Number.isFinite(updatedAt) ||
014905:             updatedAt <= 0 ||
014906:             Date.now() - updatedAt > 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
014907:         ) {
014908:             return false;
014909:         }
014910: 
014911:         const trainingCounts = entry.trainingCounts && typeof entry.trainingCounts === 'object'
014912:             ? entry.trainingCounts
014913:             : {};
014914:         return Math.max(0, parseInt(trainingCounts.critical_care, 10) || 0) >= 1;

022452:     function getSelectedTrainedPersonnelPanelModel() {
022453:         let registry = { vehicles: {} };
022454: 
022455:         try {
022456:             registry = readPersonnelTrainingRegistry();
022457:         } catch (_error) {}
022458:

022467:                         registry
022468:                     );
022469:                 } catch (_error) {}

022477:                         : []
022478:                 ).map(profile => {
022479:                     return Array.from(new Set(
022480:                         (Array.isArray(profile) ? profile : [])
022481:                             .map(value => String(value || '').trim())
022482:                             .filter(Boolean)
022483:                     ));
022484:                 }).filter(profile => profile.length > 0);
022485: 
022486:                 const trainingCounts = Object.entries(
022487:                     entry.trainingCounts &&
022488:                     typeof entry.trainingCounts === 'object'
022489:                         ? entry.trainingCounts
022490:                         : {}
022491:                 ).map(([code, value]) => {
022492:                     return {
022493:                         code: String(code || ''),
022494:                         label: getSelectedTrainingDisplayLabel(code),
022495:                         count: Math.max(0, parseInt(value, 10) || 0)
022496:                     };
022497:                 }).filter(item => item.code && item.count > 0)
022498:                     .sort((left, right) => {
022499:                         return left.label.localeCompare(right.label);
022500:                     });

022503:                     return null;

022511:                         entry.trainingProfilesComplete === true,

022455:         try {
022456:             registry = readPersonnelTrainingRegistry();

022458:

022460:             .filter(input => input?.checked)
022461:             .map(input => {
022462:                 let registryMatch = null;
022463: 
022464:                 try {
022465:                     registryMatch = getRegistryEntryForMissionCheckbox(
022466:                         input,
022467:                         registry
022468:                     );
022469:                 } catch (_error) {}
022470: 
022471:                 const entry = registryMatch?.entry || null;

022475:                     Array.isArray(entry.assignedTrainingProfiles)
022476:                         ? entry.assignedTrainingProfiles
022477:                         : []
022478:                 ).map(profile => {
022479:                     return Array.from(new Set(
022480:                         (Array.isArray(profile) ? profile : [])
022481:                             .map(value => String(value || '').trim())
022482:                             .filter(Boolean)
022483:                     ));
022484:                 }).filter(profile => profile.length > 0);
022485:

022487:                     entry.trainingCounts &&
022488:                     typeof entry.trainingCounts === 'object'
022489:                         ? entry.trainingCounts
022490:                         : {}
022491:                 ).map(([code, value]) => {
022492:                     return {
022493:                         code: String(code || ''),
022494:                         label: getSelectedTrainingDisplayLabel(code),
022495:                         count: Math.max(0, parseInt(value, 10) || 0)
022496:                     };
022497:                 }).filter(item => item.code && item.count > 0)
022498:                     .sort((left, right) => {
022499:                         return left.label.localeCompare(right.label);
022500:                     });

022503:                     return null;

022507:                     vehicleId: getMissionVehicleId(input),
022508:                     vehicleName: getVehicleDebugName(input),
022509:                     profiles,
022510:                     profilesComplete:
022511:                         entry.trainingProfilesComplete === true,
022512:                     trainingCounts
022513:                 };
022514:             })
022515:             .filter(Boolean)
022516:             .sort((left, right) => {
022517:                 return String(left.vehicleName || '')
022518:                     .localeCompare(String(right.vehicleName || ''));

022741:                     <div class="mf2026-row">
022742:                         <div class="mf2026-name">
022743:                             <span class="${cls}">${icon}</span>
022744:                             ${escapeHtml(row.originalName)}
022745:                             <div class="mf2026-small">${escapeHtml(row.mappedName)}</div>

027252:     function getSelectedTrainedPersonnelCountForCode(
027253:         selectedVehicles,
027254:         trainingCode
027255:     ) {
027256:         const code = String(trainingCode || '').trim();
027257:         if (!code) return 0;
027258: 
027259:         return (Array.isArray(selectedVehicles) ? selectedVehicles : [])
027260:             .reduce((total, vehicle) => {
027261:                 if (vehicle?.profilesComplete) {
027262:                     return total + vehicle.profiles.reduce(
027263:                         (vehicleTotal, profile) => {
027264:                             return vehicleTotal + (
027265:                                 Array.isArray(profile) &&
027266:                                 profile.includes(code)
027267:                                     ? 1
027268:                                     : 0
027269:                             );
027270:                         },
027271:                         0
027272:                     );
027273:                 }
027274: 
027275:                 const aggregate =
027276:                     (Array.isArray(vehicle?.trainingCounts)
027277:                         ? vehicle.trainingCounts
027278:                         : []
027279:                     ).find(item => item.code === code);
027280: 
027281:                 return total + Math.max(
027282:                     0,
027283:                     parseInt(aggregate?.count, 10) || 0
027284:                 );
027285:             }, 0);
027286:     }

027288:     async function preloadMissionRequiredPersonnel() {
027289:         if (
027290:             !isMissionPage() ||
027291:             !isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner(
027292:                 'required personnel preload'
027293:             )
027294:         ) {
027295:             return [];
027296:         }
027297: 
027298:         const missionKey =
027299:             synchroniseMissionRequirementPreloadCache();
027300:         if (!missionKey) return [];
027301: 
027302:         const sourceDescriptor =
027303:             getMissionRequirementSource(false);
027304:         const cachedRows =
027305:             getCachedMissionRequirementRows(sourceDescriptor);
027306: 
027307:         if (cachedRows !== null) {
027308:             return cachedRows;
027309:         }
027310: 
027311:         if (
027312:             mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromise &&
027313:             mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromiseKey === missionKey
027314:         ) {
027315:             return mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromise;
027316:         }
027317: 
027318:         mfMissionRequirementPreloadCache.status = 'loading';
027319:         mfMissionRequirementPreloadCache.attempts += 1;
027320:         mfMissionRequirementPreloadCache.error = '';
027321:         mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromiseKey = missionKey;
027322: 
027323:         const preloadPromise =
027324:             readLiveMissionRequirements({
027325:                 silent: true,
027326:                 allowCached: true,
027327:                 preload: true,
027328:                 forceRefresh: false
027329:             })
027330:                 .then(rows => {
027331:                     if (
027332:                         missionKey ===
027333:                         getMissionRequirementPreloadMissionKey()
027334:                     ) {
027335:                         renderSelectedTrainedPersonnelPanel();
027336:                     }
027337: 
027338:                     return cloneMissionRequirementRows(rows);
027339:                 })
027340:                 .catch(error => {
027341:                     if (
027342:                         missionKey ===
027343:                         getMissionRequirementPreloadMissionKey()
027344:                     ) {
027345:                         mfMissionRequirementPreloadCache.status = 'error';
027346:                         mfMissionRequirementPreloadCache.error =
027347:                             error?.message || String(error);
027348:                     }
027349: 
027350:                     return [];
027351:                 })
027352:                 .finally(() => {
027353:                     if (
027354:                         mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromise === preloadPromise
027355:                     ) {
027356:                         mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromise = null;
027357:                         mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromiseKey = '';
027358:                     }
027359: 
027360:                     const cache =
027361:                         getMissionRequirementPreloadState();
027362: 
027363:                     if (
027364:                         cache.missionKey === missionKey &&
027365:                         cache.status !== 'loaded' &&
027366:                         cache.attempts < 6
027367:                     ) {
027368:                         scheduleMissionRequiredPersonnelPreload(
027369:                             350 * cache.attempts
027370:                         );
027371:                     }
027372:                 });
027373: 
027374:         mfMissionRequirementPreloadPromise = preloadPromise;
027375:         return preloadPromise;
027376:     }

028504:     function isAuthoritativeLivePoliceTrainingEntry(
028505:         registryEntry
028506:     ) {
028507:         if (!registryEntry) return false;
028508: 
028509:         const source =
028510:             String(registryEntry.source || '');
028511:         const updatedAt =
028512:             Number(registryEntry.updatedAt || 0);
028513:         const exactRegisterEntry = !!(
028514:             source.startsWith(
028515:                 MF_EXACT_REGISTER_TRAINING_SOURCE_PREFIX
028516:             ) &&
028517:             updatedAt > 0 &&
028518:             Date.now() - updatedAt <=
028519:                 MF_EXACT_REGISTER_TRAINING_MAX_AGE_MS
028520:         );
028521: 
028522:         return !!(
028523:             (
028524:                 isStrictLiveVerifiedTrainingEntry(
028525:                     registryEntry
028526:                 ) ||
028527:                 exactRegisterEntry
028528:             ) &&
028529:             registryEntry.assignmentScanComplete === true &&
028530:             registryEntry.trainingProfilesComplete === true &&
028531:             Array.isArray(
028532:                 registryEntry.assignedTrainingProfiles
028533:             )
028534:         );
028535:     }

028558:     function isKnownProtectedTrainedPoliceIrvEntry(
028559:         registryEntry
028560:     ) {
028561:         if (!registryEntry) return false;
028562: 
028563:         const vehicleTypeId =
028564:             String(registryEntry.vehicleTypeId || '');
028565: 
028566:         // Exact MissionChief vehicle IDs are authoritative here. Older shared
028567:         // registry records may pre-date vehicle-type capture, so a missing type
028568:         // must not weaken a positive specialist-training signal.
028569:         return !!(
028570:             (!vehicleTypeId || vehicleTypeId === '8') &&
028571:             getProtectedOrdinaryIrvTrainingCount(
028572:                 registryEntry
028573:             ) > 0
028574:         );
028575:     }

011673:     function pruneLiveTrainingVerifyCache(
011674:         now = Date.now()
011675:     ) {
011676:         mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.forEach(
011677:             (verifiedAt, vehicleId) => {
011678:                 if (
011679:                     !Number.isFinite(Number(verifiedAt)) ||
011680:                     now - Number(verifiedAt) >
011681:                         MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_MS
011682:                 ) {
011683:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.delete(vehicleId);
011684:                 }
011685:             }
011686:         );
011687: 
011688:         if (
011689:             mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size >
011690:             MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_LIMIT
011691:         ) {
011692:             Array.from(mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.entries())
011693:                 .sort((left, right) => {
011694:                     return Number(left[1] || 0) - Number(right[1] || 0);
011695:                 })
011696:                 .slice(
011697:                     0,
011698:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size -
011699:                         MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_LIMIT
011700:                 )
011701:                 .forEach(([vehicleId]) => {
011702:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.delete(vehicleId);
011703:                 });
011704:         }
011705:

011673:     function pruneLiveTrainingVerifyCache(
011674:         now = Date.now()
011675:     ) {
011676:         mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.forEach(
011677:             (verifiedAt, vehicleId) => {
011678:                 if (
011679:                     !Number.isFinite(Number(verifiedAt)) ||
011680:                     now - Number(verifiedAt) >
011681:                         MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_MS
011682:                 ) {
011683:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.delete(vehicleId);
011684:                 }
011685:             }
011686:         );
011687: 
011688:         if (
011689:             mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size >
011690:             MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_LIMIT
011691:         ) {
011692:             Array.from(mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.entries())
011693:                 .sort((left, right) => {
011694:                     return Number(left[1] || 0) - Number(right[1] || 0);
011695:                 })
011696:                 .slice(
011697:                     0,
011698:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size -
011699:                         MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_LIMIT
011700:                 )
011701:                 .forEach(([vehicleId]) => {
011702:                     mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.delete(vehicleId);
011703:                 });
011704:         }
011705: 
011706:         return mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size;
